            lastPos = nil,
            smoothVel = Vector3.zero,
            velHist = {},
            accelHist = {},
            airHist = {},
            prevDir = nil,
            lastDirChange = 0,
            airborneTime = 0,
            lastTarget = nil,
        }
    end

    local function push(hist, v, n)
        hist[#hist + 1] = v
        while #hist > n do table.remove(hist, 1) end
    end

    local function avg(hist)
        if #hist == 0 then return Vector3.zero end
        local s = Vector3.zero
        for i = 1, #hist do s = s + hist[i] end
        return s / #hist
    end

    local function predictTarget(tr, target, dt)
        dt = math.clamp(dt or 0.016, 0.001, 0.05)
        local tPos = target.Position
        local rawVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
        if tr.lastPos then
            local derived = (tPos - tr.lastPos) / dt
            if derived.Magnitude < 220 then
                rawVel = rawVel:Lerp(derived, 0.4)
            end
        end
        tr.lastPos = tPos
        if rawVel.Magnitude > 160 then rawVel = rawVel.Unit * 160 end

        local prev = tr.smoothVel
        tr.smoothVel = tr.smoothVel:Lerp(rawVel, 0.32)
        push(tr.velHist, tr.smoothVel, 10)
        local accel = (tr.smoothVel - prev) / dt
        if accel.Magnitude > 350 then accel = accel.Unit * 350 end
        push(tr.accelHist, accel, 7)

        local tHum = target.Parent and target.Parent:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        local airborne = tHum and tHum.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
        if airborne then
            tr.airborneTime = tr.airborneTime + dt
            if tr.airborneTime >= 0.06 then push(tr.airHist, tr.smoothVel, 7) end
        else
            tr.airborneTime = 0
            tr.airHist = {}
        end

        local flatV = Vector3.new(tr.smoothVel.X, 0, tr.smoothVel.Z)
        local quickTurn = false
        if flatV.Magnitude > 4 then
            local dir = flatV.Unit
            if tr.prevDir and tr.prevDir:Dot(dir) < 0.4 then
                quickTurn = (os.clock() - tr.lastDirChange) < 0.15
                tr.lastDirChange = os.clock()
            end
            tr.prevDir = dir
        end

        local ping = pingSec()
        local delay = ping + (1 / 60)
        if quickTurn then delay = delay * 1.5 end
        delay = math.clamp(delay, 0.035, 0.45)

        local predVel = tr.smoothVel
        if airborne and #tr.airHist > 0 then
            local a = avg(tr.airHist)
            predVel = Vector3.new(a.X, tr.smoothVel.Y, a.Z) * 0.88
        end

        local pred = tPos + predVel * delay
        pred = pred + avg(tr.accelHist) * 0.32 * (delay * delay * 0.5)
        if airborne then
            local pt = 0.2
            pred = pred + predVel * pt + Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * 196.2 * pt * pt, 0)
        end
        pcall(function() pred = pred + target.CFrame.LookVector * 0.6 end)
        return pred, predVel, flatV, airborne
    end

    local function resetTracker(tr, target)
        if tr.lastTarget ~= target then
            tr.lastPos, tr.smoothVel = nil, Vector3.zero
            tr.velHist, tr.accelHist, tr.airHist = {}, {}, {}
            tr.prevDir, tr.airborneTime = nil, 0
            tr.lastTarget = target
        end
    end

    -- ═══════════════ AIMBOT (Rose X logic) ═══════════════
    function AdaptBat.StartAimbot()
        AdaptBat.StopAimbot()
        AdaptBat.aimbotOn = true
        AdaptBat.stickyTarget = nil
        local _, _, hum0 = getChar()
        if hum0 then
            AdaptBat._aimSavedAR = hum0.AutoRotate
            hum0.AutoRotate = false
        end

        AdaptBat.conns.aimbot = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not AdaptBat.aimbotOn then return end
            local char, root, hum = getChar()
            if not char or not root or not hum then return end

            if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
                local bat = findBatTool(char)
                if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
            end

            local target = closestEnemyRoot(280, 20)
            if not target then return end

            local targetVel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            local myPos = root.Position
            local targetPos = target.Position
            -- Rose X prediction
            local predictPos = targetPos + targetVel * 0.14
            predictPos = predictPos + target.CFrame.LookVector * 0.3
            local direction = predictPos - myPos
            local flatDir = Vector3.new(direction.X, 0, direction.Z)
            if flatDir.Magnitude > 0 then flatDir = flatDir.Unit else flatDir = Vector3.zero end

            local desiredHeight = targetPos.Y + 3.7
            local yVel = (desiredHeight - myPos.Y) * 19.5 + targetVel.Y * 0.8
            if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then yVel = math.max(yVel, 13) end
            yVel = math.clamp(yVel, -70, 110)

            local spd = AdaptBat.aimSpeed or 58
            local desiredVel = Vector3.new(flatDir.X * spd, yVel, flatDir.Z * spd)
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(desiredVel, 0.8)
            AdaptBat.intendedVel = desiredVel

            local speed3 = targetVel.Magnitude
            local predictTime = math.clamp(speed3 / 150, 0.05, 0.2)
            local predictedPos = targetPos + targetVel * predictTime
            local toPredict = predictedPos - myPos
            if toPredict.Magnitude > 0.1 then
                local goalCF = CFrame.lookAt(myPos, predictedPos)
                local diffCF = root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF
                local rx, ry, rz = diffCF:ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
                rx = math.clamp(rx, -2.5, 2.5)
                ry = math.clamp(ry, -2.5, 2.5)
                rz = math.clamp(rz, -2.5, 2.5)
                root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(Vector3.new(rx * 42, ry * 42, rz * 42))
            end

            local distToTarget = (root.Position - target.Position).Magnitude
            if distToTarget <= 8 then
                swingBat(char, hum, target)
            end
        end)
    end

    function AdaptBat.StopAimbot()
        AdaptBat.aimbotOn = false
        clearConn("aimbot")
        local _, root, hum = getChar()
        if hum then
            hum.AutoRotate = (AdaptBat._aimSavedAR ~= nil) and AdaptBat._aimSavedAR or true
            hum.PlatformStand = false
            pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
        end
        if root then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, -0.1, 0)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        AdaptBat._aimSavedAR = nil
        AdaptBat.stickyTarget = nil
    end

    -- ═══════════════ BYPASS (Rose X SnowVS.BatBypass / BB.Start logic) ═══════════════
    local function bbAvg(hist)
        if #hist == 0 then return Vector3.zero end
        local total = Vector3.zero
        for _, v in ipairs(hist) do total = total + v end
        return total / #hist
    end

    local function bbPush(hist, value, maximum)
        table.insert(hist, value)
        if #hist > maximum then table.remove(hist, 1) end
    end

    function AdaptBat.StartBypass()
        AdaptBat.StopBypass()
        AdaptBat.StopAimbot()
        AdaptBat.bypassOn = true
        AdaptBat.stickyTarget = nil

        local followSpeed = (type(AdaptBat.bypassSpeed) == "number" and AdaptBat.bypassSpeed > 0) and AdaptBat.bypassSpeed or 56
        local ZCFG = {
            FOLLOW_SPEED = followSpeed,
            ACTIVATE_DISTANCE = AdaptBat.hitDist or 13,
            MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE = 1,
            PREDICTION_TIME = 0.22,
            PREDICT_AHEAD = 3,
            MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE = 150,
            VELOCITY_SMOOTHING = 0.2,
            MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY = 80,
            SERVER_TICKRATE = 1 / 60,
            MIN_PING_COMPENSATION = 0.03,
            MAX_PING_COMPENSATION = 0.25,
            ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT = 0.3,
            DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME = 0.12,
            QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER = 1.5,
            GRAVITY = 196.2,
            AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR = 0.8,
            MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME = 0.08,
        }

        local Z = {
            targetPlayer = nil,
            lastTargetPos = nil,
            targetVelocity = Vector3.zero,
            smoothedVelocity = Vector3.zero,
            velocityHistory = {},
            accelerationHistory = {},
            aerialVelocityHistory = {},
            previousDirection = nil,
            lastDirectionChangeTime = 0,
            airborneTime = 0,
            lastActivationTime = 0,
            currentPing = 0.1,
            realPingMs = 0,
        }
        AdaptBat._bbZ = Z
        AdaptBat._bbZCFG = ZCFG

        local _, _, hum0 = getChar()
        if hum0 then
            AdaptBat._bpSavedAR = hum0.AutoRotate
            hum0.AutoRotate = false
        end

        -- ping sampler
        AdaptBat.conns.bypassPing = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            -- sampled inside loop via GetNetworkPing; keep lightweight
        end)

        AdaptBat.conns.bypass = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
            if not AdaptBat.bypassOn then return end
            dt = math.clamp(dt or 0.016, 0.001, 0.05)
            local char, root, hum = getChar()
            if not char or not root or not hum then return end

            -- refresh speed (user set 56)
            ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED = (type(AdaptBat.bypassSpeed) == "number" and AdaptBat.bypassSpeed > 0) and AdaptBat.bypassSpeed or 56
            if _G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed then
                local s = _G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
                if type(s) == "number" and s > 0 then ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED = s end
            end

            local bat = findBatTool(char)
            if bat and bat.Parent ~= char then
                pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end)
            end

            -- nearest target
            local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, math.huge
            for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                if candidate ~= LP and candidate.Character then
                    local targetRoot = candidate.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                    local targetHumanoid = candidate.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
                    if targetRoot and targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                        local distance = (root.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
                        if distance < nearestDistance then
                            nearest, nearestDistance = candidate, distance
                        end
                    end
                end
            end

            if not nearest or not nearest.Character then
                Z.targetPlayer = nil
                Z.lastTargetPos = nil
                return
            end

            local targetRoot = nearest.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHumanoid = nearest.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not targetRoot or not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
                return
            end

            -- velocity tracking
            local targetPos = targetRoot.Position
            local instantVel = targetRoot.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            if Z.lastTargetPos then
                local derived = (targetPos - Z.lastTargetPos) / dt
                if derived.Magnitude < ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE * 2 then
                    instantVel = instantVel:Lerp(derived, 0.35)
                end
            end
            Z.lastTargetPos = targetPos

            local horiz = Vector3.new(instantVel.X, 0, instantVel.Z)
            if horiz.Magnitude > ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then
                horiz = horiz.Unit * ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY
                instantVel = Vector3.new(horiz.X, instantVel.Y, horiz.Z)
            end

            local prevSmooth = Z.smoothedVelocity
            Z.smoothedVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(instantVel, ZCFG.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)
            Z.targetVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity
            bbPush(Z.velocityHistory, Z.smoothedVelocity, 8)

            local accel = (Z.smoothedVelocity - prevSmooth) / dt
            if accel.Magnitude > 300 then accel = accel.Unit * 300 end
            bbPush(Z.accelerationHistory, accel, 6)

            local airborne = targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
            Z.airborneTime = airborne and (Z.airborneTime + dt) or 0
            if airborne and Z.airborneTime >= ZCFG.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then
                bbPush(Z.aerialVelocityHistory, Z.targetVelocity, 6)
            elseif not airborne then
                Z.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
            end

            local predictionVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity
            if airborne and #Z.aerialVelocityHistory > 0 then
                local aerial = bbAvg(Z.aerialVelocityHistory)
                predictionVelocity = Vector3.new(aerial.X, Z.targetVelocity.Y, aerial.Z) * ZCFG.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR
            end

            local quickTurn = false
            local horizontalVelocity = Vector3.new(Z.targetVelocity.X, 0, Z.targetVelocity.Z)
            if horizontalVelocity.Magnitude > 5 then
                local direction = horizontalVelocity.Unit
                if Z.previousDirection and Z.previousDirection:Dot(direction) < 0.5 then
                    quickTurn = (os.clock() - Z.lastDirectionChangeTime) < ZCFG.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME
                    Z.lastDirectionChangeTime = os.clock()
                end
                Z.previousDirection = direction
            end

            -- ping
            local okPing, pingVal = pcall(function() return LP:GetNetworkPing() end)
            if okPing and type(pingVal) == "number" then
                Z.currentPing = math.clamp(pingVal, ZCFG.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION, ZCFG.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION)
            end

            local serverDelay = Z.currentPing + ZCFG.SERVER_TICKRATE
            if quickTurn then serverDelay = serverDelay * ZCFG.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER end

            local predicted = targetPos + predictionVelocity * serverDelay
            local acceleration = bbAvg(Z.accelerationHistory)
            predicted = predicted + acceleration * ZCFG.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT * (serverDelay * serverDelay * 0.5)

            local predictionTime = ZCFG.PREDICTION_TIME * 1.1
            if airborne then
                predicted = predicted + predictionVelocity * predictionTime
                predicted = predicted + Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * ZCFG.GRAVITY * predictionTime * predictionTime, 0)
            else
                predicted = predicted + predictionVelocity * predictionTime
            end

            local flatPrediction = Vector3.new(predictionVelocity.X, 0, predictionVelocity.Z)
            if flatPrediction.Magnitude > 1 then
                predicted = predicted + flatPrediction.Unit * ZCFG.PREDICT_AHEAD
            end

            local toTarget = predicted - root.Position

            -- rotate toward target (Rose X bbRotateRoot style)
            hum.AutoRotate = false
            if toTarget.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                local dir = toTarget.Unit
                local axis = root.CFrame.LookVector:Cross(dir)
                local angle = math.asin(math.clamp(axis.Magnitude, -1, 1))
                if axis.Magnitude > 0.01 then
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = axis.Unit * angle * 80
                else
                    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                end
                local face = Vector3.new(predicted.X, root.Position.Y, predicted.Z)
                if (face - root.Position).Magnitude > 0.05 then
                    root.CFrame = CFrame.lookAt(root.Position, face)
                end
            end

            -- swing
            if (targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude <= ZCFG.ACTIVATE_DISTANCE
                and (os.clock() - Z.lastActivationTime) >= 0.3 then
                if bat then pcall(function() bat:Activate() end) end
                local bat2 = findBatTool(char)
                if bat2 then
                    pcall(function()
                        if bat2.Parent ~= char then hum:EquipTool(bat2) end
                        bat2:Activate()
                        local ev = bat2:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
                        if ev then ev:FireServer() end
                    end)
                end
                Z.lastActivationTime = os.clock()
            end

            if toTarget.Magnitude > ZCFG.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = toTarget.Unit * ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED
                AdaptBat.intendedVel = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity
            else
                root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 0.5, 0)
            end
        end)
    end

    function AdaptBat.StopBypass()
        AdaptBat.bypassOn = false
        clearConn("bypass")
        clearConn("bypassPing")
        clearConn("bypassSafety")
        clearConn("bypassRespawn")
        local _, root, hum = getChar()
        if hum then
            hum.AutoRotate = (AdaptBat._bpSavedAR ~= nil) and AdaptBat._bpSavedAR or true
        end
        if root then
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        AdaptBat._bpSavedAR = nil
        AdaptBat.stickyTarget = nil
        AdaptBat._bbZ = nil
    end

    -- ═══════════════ TP BAT ═══════════════
    local function hardTpTo(root, hum, target)
        if not root or not target or not target.Parent then return end
        local vel = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity or Vector3.zero
        local height = 1.0
        local pos
        if AdaptBat.tpMode == "HIGH PING" then
            local ping = pingSec()
            local pred = Vector3.new(vel.X, vel.Y * 0.25, vel.Z) * math.clamp(ping * 1.2 + 0.05, 0.05, 0.2)
            pos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, height, 0) + pred
        else
            pos = target.Position + Vector3.new(0, height, 0)
        end
        local look = target.Position
        local head = target.Parent:FindFirstChild("Head")
        if head then look = head.Position end

        pcall(function()
            if hum then
                hum.AutoRotate = false
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                hum.Sit = false
                hum:Move(Vector3.zero)
                local st = hum:GetState()
                if st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Physics or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Ragdoll
                    or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.FallingDown or st == Enum.HumanoidStateType.PlatformStanding then
                    hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.GettingUp)
                end
            end
            pcall(function() if root.SetNetworkOwner then root:SetNetworkOwner(LP) end end)
            setHidden(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", target)

            local cf = CFrame.lookAt(pos, Vector3.new(look.X, pos.Y, look.Z))
            root.CFrame = cf
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
            pcall(function()
                root.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                root.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
            end)
            -- triple write against server reconciliation
            root.CFrame = cf
            task.defer(function()
                if AdaptBat.tpBatOn and root.Parent then
                    pcall(function() root.CFrame = cf end)
                end
            end)
        end)
    end

    function AdaptBat.StartTpBat(mode)
        AdaptBat.StopTpBat()
        AdaptBat.tpMode = (mode == "LOW PING" or mode == "V2" or mode == "V3") and "LOW PING" or "HIGH PING"
        AdaptBat.tpBatOn = true
        AdaptBat.stickyTarget = nil
        AdaptBat.lastSwing = 0
        local _, _, hum0 = getChar()
        if hum0 then
            AdaptBat._tpSavedWS = hum0.WalkSpeed
            AdaptBat._tpSavedJP = hum0.JumpPower
            AdaptBat._tpSavedAR = hum0.AutoRotate
        end

        AdaptBat.conns.tpBat = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
            if not AdaptBat.tpBatOn then return end
            local char, root, hum = getChar()
            if not char or not root or not hum then return end
            local target = closestEnemyRoot(320, 35)
            if not target then
                setHidden(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil)
                return
            end
            hardTpTo(root, hum, target)
            ensureBat(char, hum)
            if AdaptBat.cameraSteer then
                local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
                if cam then
                    pcall(function()
                        cam.CFrame = CFrame.new(cam.CFrame.Position, target.Position + Vector3.new(0, 1.2, 0))
                    end)
                end
            end
            swingBat(char, hum, target)
        end)

        AdaptBat.conns.tpBatHb = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            if not AdaptBat.tpBatOn then return end
            local char, root, hum = getChar()
            if not root then return end
            local target = closestEnemyRoot(320, 35)
            if target then
                hardTpTo(root, hum, target)
                if char then swingBat(char, hum, target) end
            end
        end)
    end

    function AdaptBat.StopTpBat()
        AdaptBat.tpBatOn = false
        clearConn("tpBat")
        clearConn("tpBatHb")
        local _, root, hum = getChar()
        if root then
            setHidden(root, "PhysicsRepRootPart", nil)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
        end
        if hum then
            pcall(function()
                hum.AutoRotate = (AdaptBat._tpSavedAR ~= nil) and AdaptBat._tpSavedAR or true
                hum.PlatformStand = false
                if AdaptBat._tpSavedWS and AdaptBat._tpSavedWS > 0 then hum.WalkSpeed = AdaptBat._tpSavedWS end
                if AdaptBat._tpSavedJP then hum.JumpPower = AdaptBat._tpSavedJP end
            end)
        end
        AdaptBat._tpSavedWS, AdaptBat._tpSavedJP, AdaptBat._tpSavedAR = nil, nil, nil
        AdaptBat.stickyTarget = nil
    end

    function AdaptBat.StopAll()
        AdaptBat.StopAimbot()
        AdaptBat.StopBypass()
        AdaptBat.StopTpBat()
    end

    LP.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
        task.wait(0.35)
        if AdaptBat.aimbotOn then AdaptBat.StartAimbot() end
        if AdaptBat.bypassOn then AdaptBat.StartBypass() end
        if AdaptBat.tpBatOn then AdaptBat.StartTpBat(AdaptBat.tpMode) end
    end)

    print("[AdaptBatCore] v2 loaded")
end


-- ===== Bat Aimbot Bypass helpers (ported) =====
_RX_BYPASS_HIT_DIST = 11
_RX_BYPASS_SWING_CD = 0.07
_rxBypassLocked = nil
_rxBypassHitCD = false
function _rxBypassClosest()
    local char = LP.Character
    local myRoot = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myRoot then return nil, math.huge end
    -- Sticky targeting: keep the current target unless someone is clearly closer,
    -- so the aimbot stops flip-flopping between two nearby players.
    local best, bestD, bestScore, bestPlr = nil, math.huge, math.huge, nil
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local c = plr ~= LP and plr.Character
        local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local h = c and c:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if r and h and h.Health > 0 then
            local d = (r.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude
            local score = d
            if plr == _rxBypassLocked then score = d - 18 end
            if score < bestScore then bestScore, bestD, best, bestPlr = score, d, r, plr end
        end
    end
    _rxBypassLocked = bestPlr
    return best, bestD
end
function _rxBypassSwing()
    if _rxBypassHitCD then return end
    _rxBypassHitCD = true
    pcall(function()
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local bat = findBat and findBat() or nil
        if not bat then
            -- fallback: any bat/slap tool
            for _, t in ipairs(char:GetChildren()) do
                if t:IsA("Tool") then
                    local n = t.Name:lower()
                    if n:find("bat", 1, true) or n:find("slap", 1, true) then bat = t; break end
                end
            end
            if not bat then
                local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
                if bp then
                    for _, t in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
                        if t:IsA("Tool") then
                            local n = t.Name:lower()
                            if n:find("bat", 1, true) or n:find("slap", 1, true) then bat = t; break end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
        if not bat then return end
        if bat.Parent ~= char then
            local h = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if h then pcall(function() h:EquipTool(bat) end) end
        end
        pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
        local remote = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            or bat:FindFirstChild("RemoteEvent")
            or bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteFunction")
        if remote and remote:IsA("RemoteEvent") then pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end) end
        local handle = bat:FindFirstChild("Handle")
        local myRoot = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        if handle and firetouchinterest and myRoot then
            local hitR = _RX_BYPASS_HIT_DIST + 4
            for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
                local c = plr ~= LP and plr.Character
                local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
                if r and (r.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude <= hitR then
                    for _, part in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
                        if part:IsA("BasePart") then
                            pcall(function()
                                firetouchinterest(handle, part, 0)
                                firetouchinterest(handle, part, 1)
                            end)
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
        end
    end)
    task.delay(_RX_BYPASS_SWING_CD, function() _rxBypassHitCD = false end)
end

function stopAimbotAdapt()
    if _G.AdaptBatCore then pcall(function() _G.AdaptBatCore.StopAimbot() end) end
    if _aimbotConn then
        pcall(function() _aimbotConn:Disconnect() end)
        _aimbotConn = nil
    end
    local char = LP.Character
    local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum then
        hum.AutoRotate = (_prevAutoRotate == nil) and true or _prevAutoRotate
        hum.PlatformStand = false
        pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Running) end)
    end
    if root then
        root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = _V3new(0, -0.1, 0)
        root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = _V3zero
    end
    _prevAutoRotate = nil
    lastMoveDir = _V3zero
    _unsuppressBodyLock(true)
end

-- True velocity from position samples (beats spoofed AssemblyLinearVelocity)
local _BatVTrack = { records = {} }
local function _batGetTrueVel(root, now)
    if not root or not root.Parent then return _V3zero end
    local rec = _BatVTrack.records[root]
    if not rec then
        rec = { s = {}, vel = _V3zero, ready = false }
        _BatVTrack.records[root] = rec
    end
    local pos = root.Position
    local last = rec.s[#rec.s]
    if last then
        local dt = now - last.t
        if dt > 1/240 and dt < 0.2 then
            local raw = (pos - last.pos) / dt
            local c = 160
            local clamped = _V3new(_clamp(raw.X, -c, c), _clamp(raw.Y, -c, c), _clamp(raw.Z, -c, c))
            if rec.ready then
                rec.vel = rec.vel:Lerp(clamped, 0.55)
            else
                rec.vel = clamped
                rec.ready = true
            end
        end
    end
    table.insert(rec.s, { pos = pos, t = now })
    while #rec.s > 7 do table.remove(rec.s, 1) end
    return rec.vel
end
local function _batReadVel(target, now)
    local raw = target.AssemblyLinearVelocity
    local tracked = _batGetTrueVel(target, now)
    if not tracked or tracked.Magnitude < 0.4 then
        if raw.Magnitude > 400 then return _V3zero end
        return raw
    end
    local diff = (raw - tracked).Magnitude
    if raw.Magnitude > 220 then return tracked end
    if diff > math.max(70, tracked.Magnitude * 1.4) then return tracked end
    return raw
end
local _batVtCleanAt = 0
local function _batVTrackCleanup(now)
    if now - _batVtCleanAt < 4 then return end
    _batVtCleanAt = now
    for r in pairs(_BatVTrack.records) do
        if not r.Parent then _BatVTrack.records[r] = nil end
    end
end

function startAimbotAdapt()
    if _G.AdaptBatCore then
        _G.AdaptBatCore.aimSpeed = (type(BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED)=="number" and BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 62
        _G.AdaptBatCore.swingDelay = 0.007
        _G.AdaptBatCore.StartAimbot()
        return
    end
    if _aimbotConn then return end
    _suppressBodyLock()
    _rxBypassLocked = nil
    _BatVTrack.records = {}
    local hum0 = LP.Character and LP.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if hum0 then
        if _prevAutoRotate == nil then _prevAutoRotate = hum0.AutoRotate end
        hum0.AutoRotate = false
    end
    _aimbotConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not autoBatEnabled then return end
        local char = LP.Character
        if not char then return end
        local root = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not root or not hum then return end
        if not char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") then
            local bat = findBat()
            if bat then pcall(function() hum:EquipTool(bat) end) end
        end
        local bTarget, bDist = _rxBypassClosest()
        if not bTarget then return end
        local now = os.clock()
        _batVTrackCleanup(now)
        local myPos = root.Position
        local tPos = bTarget.Position
        local tVel = _batReadVel(bTarget, now)
        local tFlatVel = _V3new(tVel.X, 0, tVel.Z)
        if tFlatVel.Magnitude > 95 then tFlatVel = tFlatVel.Unit * 95 end
        -- Strong lead: ping + travel + look-ahead on facing
        local spd = (type(BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED) == "number" and BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED > 0) and BAT_AIMBOT_SPEED or 60
        local travel = _clamp(bDist / math.max(spd, 1), 0, 0.42)
        local lead = _clamp(_rxPing() + travel * 0.8, 0.04, 0.38)
        local predPos = tPos + tVel * lead
        pcall(function()
            predPos = predPos + bTarget.CFrame.LookVector * 0.45
        end)
        local delta = predPos - myPos
        local flatDelta = _V3new(delta.X, 0, delta.Z)
        local flatDist = flatDelta.Magnitude
        local flat = (flatDist > 0.05) and flatDelta.Unit or _V3zero
        local yVel = ((tPos.Y + 3.7) - myPos.Y) * 21 + tVel.Y * 0.85
        if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then yVel = math.max(yVel, 14) end
        yVel = _clamp(yVel, -80, 120)
        -- Far: hard chase. Close: stick to target velocity so we don't overshoot.
        local approach = _clamp(flatDist / 4, 0.35, 1)
        local chase = spd * approach
        local desired = flat * chase + tFlatVel * (1 - approach) * 0.55
        -- Hard set velocity so CarrySystem / other movers cannot soft-block aimbot
        local lerpA = (flatDist < 12) and 1 or 0.9
        local goalVel = _V3new(desired.X, yVel, desired.Z)
        if lerpA >= 1 then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = goalVel
        else
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity:Lerp(goalVel, lerpA)
        end
        -- Also push LinearVelocity constraint if present so it doesn't cancel us
        pcall(function()
            local lv = root:FindFirstChild("CarryBoostLV")
            if lv and lv:IsA("LinearVelocity") then
                lv.Enabled = false
            end
        end)
        if delta.Magnitude > 0.1 then
            local goalCF = _CFlookAt(myPos, predPos)
            local rx, ry, rz = (root.CFrame:Inverse() * goalCF):ToEulerAnglesXYZ()
            rx, ry, rz = _clamp(rx, -2.8, 2.8), _clamp(ry, -2.8, 2.8), _clamp(rz, -2.8, 2.8)
            root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = root.CFrame:VectorToWorldSpace(_V3new(rx * 52, ry * 52, rz * 52))
        end
        local hitPad = _clamp(tFlatVel.Magnitude * 0.14, 0, 5)
        if bDist <= _RX_BYPASS_HIT_DIST + hitPad then
            _rxBypassSwing()
        end
    end)
end

function disableAutoBat()
    autoBatEnabled = false
    if CarrySystem then CarrySystem._batAimbotToggled = false end
    if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
    if mobSetAutoBat then pcall(mobSetAutoBat, false) end
    pcall(stopAimbotAdapt)
    if _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn and _G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot then
        pcall(_G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot)
    end
    if BB and BB.Stop then pcall(BB.Stop) end
end

function enableAutoBat()
    -- Only hard-block when safe mode is actively locked
    if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked and _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked() then
        autoBatEnabled = false
        if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(false) end
        if mobSetAutoBat then pcall(mobSetAutoBat, false) end
        return
    end
    if autoLeftEnabled then
        autoLeftEnabled = false
        if autoLeftSetVisual then autoLeftSetVisual(false) end
        pcall(stopAutoLeft)
    end
    if autoRightEnabled then
        autoRightEnabled = false
        if autoRightSetVisual then autoRightSetVisual(false) end
        pcall(stopAutoRight)
    end
    if batDesyncTpEnabled then pcall(toggleBatDesyncTp) end
    if autoBatV2Enabled then pcall(disableBatV2) end
    autoBatEnabled = true
    if CarrySystem then CarrySystem._batAimbotToggled = true end
    if autoBatSetVisual then autoBatSetVisual(true) end
    if mobSetAutoBat then pcall(mobSetAutoBat, true) end
    local mode = tostring(selectedAimbotMode or "Normal")
    if mode == "Bat Bypass" or mode == "Bypass" or mode == "Anti Bypass" then
        if _G.AceStartAntiBypassAimbot then
            pcall(_G.AceStartAntiBypassAimbot)
        elseif BB and BB.Start then
            pcall(BB.Start)
        else
            pcall(startAimbotAdapt)
        end
    else
        pcall(stopAimbotAdapt)
        pcall(startAimbotAdapt)
    end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- BAT BYPASS — Persecución autónoma con predicción de ping
-- (Reemplaza el módulo Anti Bypass anterior. Antes BAT V2.)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

BAT_V2_SPEED           = BAT_V2_SPEED           or 55
BAT_V2_HIT_DIST        = BAT_V2_HIT_DIST        or 13
BAT_V2_LEAD_STUDS      = BAT_V2_LEAD_STUDS      or 3
BAT_V2_BODY_LOCK_RANGE = BAT_V2_BODY_LOCK_RANGE or 60

local SnowVS = _G.SnowVS or {}
_G.SnowVS = SnowVS

SnowVS.BatBypass = SnowVS.BatBypass or {}
-- Alias legado para no romper referencias externas existentes
SnowVS.AimbotBypassV2 = SnowVS.BatBypass

local BB = SnowVS.BatBypass

BB.Enabled    = BB.Enabled    or false
BB.Connection = BB.Connection or nil

BB.Z = BB.Z or {
    targetPlayer          = nil,
    lastTargetPos         = nil,
    targetVelocity        = Vector3.zero,
    smoothedVelocity      = Vector3.zero,
    velocityHistory       = {},
    accelerationHistory   = {},
    aerialVelocityHistory = {},
    previousDirection     = nil,
    lastDirectionChangeTime = 0,
    airborneTime          = 0,
    lastActivationTime    = 0,
    currentPing           = 0.1,
    realPingMs            = 0,
}

BB.ZCFG = BB.ZCFG or {
    FOLLOW_SPEED                    = BAT_V2_SPEED,
    ACTIVATE_DISTANCE               = BAT_V2_HIT_DIST,
    MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE             = 1,
    PREDICTION_TIME                 = 0.22,
    PREDICT_AHEAD                   = BAT_V2_LEAD_STUDS,
    MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE             = 150,
    VELOCITY_SMOOTHING              = 0.2,
    MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY         = 80,
    SERVER_TICKRATE                 = 1 / 60,
    MIN_PING_COMPENSATION           = 0.03,
    MAX_PING_COMPENSATION           = 0.25,
    ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT  = 0.3,
    DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME = 0.12,
    QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER = 1.5,
    GRAVITY                         = 196.2,
    AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR              = 0.8,
    MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME               = 0.08,
}

local function bbAverageVector(history)
    if #history == 0 then return Vector3.zero end
    local total = Vector3.zero
    for _, v in ipairs(history) do total += v end
    return total / #history
end

local function bbPushHistory(history, value, maximum)
    table.insert(history, value)
    if #history > maximum then table.remove(history, 1) end
end

function BB.FindBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    local equipped = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool")
    if equipped then
        local n = equipped.Name:lower()
        if n:find("bat", 1, true) or n:find("slap", 1, true) then
            return equipped
        end
    end
    local bp = LP:FindFirstChildOfClass("Backpack")
    if bp then
        for _, tool in ipairs(bp:GetChildren()) do
            if tool:IsA("Tool") then
                local n = tool.Name:lower()
                if n:find("bat", 1, true) or n:find("slap", 1, true) then
                    return tool
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nil
end

local function bbResetTarget()
    local Z = BB.Z
    Z.targetPlayer          = nil
    Z.lastTargetPos         = nil
    Z.targetVelocity        = Vector3.zero
    Z.smoothedVelocity      = Vector3.zero
    Z.velocityHistory       = {}
    Z.accelerationHistory   = {}
    Z.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
    Z.previousDirection     = nil
    Z.airborneTime          = 0
end

local function bbNearestTarget(root)
    local nearest, nearestDistance = nil, math.huge
    for _, candidate in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if candidate ~= LP and candidate.Character then
            local targetRoot     = candidate.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            local targetHumanoid = candidate.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if targetRoot and targetHumanoid and targetHumanoid.Health > 0 then
                local distance = (root.Position - targetRoot.Position).Magnitude
                if distance < nearestDistance then
                    nearest, nearestDistance = candidate, distance
                end
            end
        end
    end
    return nearest
end

local function bbRotateRoot(root, direction)
    if direction.Magnitude < 0.01 then return end
    local axis  = root.CFrame.LookVector:Cross(direction.Unit)
    local angle = math.asin(math.clamp(axis.Magnitude, -1, 1))
    root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = axis.Magnitude > 0.01
        and axis.Unit * angle * 80
        or  Vector3.zero
end

local function bbSamplePing()
    local ok, value = pcall(function()
        return game:GetService("Stats").Network.ServerStatsItem["Data Ping"]:GetValue()
    end)
    local Z = BB.Z
    if ok and type(value) == "number" then Z.realPingMs = math.floor(value) end
    Z.currentPing = math.clamp(
        Z.realPingMs / 1000,
        BB.ZCFG.MIN_PING_COMPENSATION,
        BB.ZCFG.MAX_PING_COMPENSATION
    )
end

BB.PingLoopStarted = BB.PingLoopStarted or false
if not BB.PingLoopStarted then
    BB.PingLoopStarted = true
    task.spawn(function()
        while true do
            pcall(bbSamplePing)
            task.wait(0.5)
        end
    end)
end

-- Stubs para compatibilidad con la API antigua (Body Lock ya no se usa aparte)
function BB.StartBodyLock() end
function BB.StopBodyLock()  end

function BB.Stop()
    local Z = BB.Z
    BB.Enabled = false
    if BB.Connection then
        BB.Connection:Disconnect()
        BB.Connection = nil
    end
    local char     = LP.Character
    local humanoid = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local root     = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if humanoid then humanoid.AutoRotate = true end
    if root     then root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero end
    bbResetTarget()
    Z.lastActivationTime = 0
end

function BB.Start()
    if BB.Connection then
        BB.Enabled = true
        return
    end

    BB.Enabled = true

    BB.Connection = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function(dt)
        if not BB.Enabled then BB.Stop(); return end
        local Z     = BB.Z
        local ZCFG  = BB.ZCFG

        -- Refresca velocidad según modo actual (Normal/Lagger/Lagger Carry)
        if _G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed then
            ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED = _G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed()
        end

        local currentChar     = LP.Character
        local currentRoot     = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local currentHumanoid = currentChar and currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not currentRoot or not currentHumanoid or currentHumanoid.Health <= 0 then return end

        currentHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
        root, humanoid = currentRoot, currentHumanoid

        bat = currentChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Tool") or BB.FindBat()
        if bat and bat.Parent ~= currentChar then
            pcall(currentHumanoid.EquipTool, currentHumanoid, bat)
        end

        Z.targetPlayer  = bbNearestTarget(root)
        local targetChar = Z.targetPlayer and Z.targetPlayer.Character
        local targetRoot = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
        local targetHumanoid = targetChar and targetChar:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
        if not targetRoot or not targetHumanoid or targetHumanoid.Health <= 0 then
            bbResetTarget(); return
        end

        local targetPos = targetRoot.Position
        local safeDt    = math.max(dt, 1 / 240)

        -- Estima velocidad del rival filtrando outliers
        if Z.lastTargetPos then
            local rawVelocity   = (targetPos - Z.lastTargetPos) / safeDt
            local velocityDelta = rawVelocity - Z.targetVelocity
            if velocityDelta.Magnitude > ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE then
                rawVelocity = Z.targetVelocity + velocityDelta.Unit * ZCFG.MAX_VELOCITY_CHANGE
            end
            local horizontal = Vector3.new(rawVelocity.X, 0, rawVelocity.Z)
            if horizontal.Magnitude > ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY then
                horizontal  = horizontal.Unit * ZCFG.MAX_HORIZONTAL_VELOCITY
                rawVelocity = Vector3.new(horizontal.X, rawVelocity.Y, horizontal.Z)
            end
            bbPushHistory(Z.accelerationHistory, (rawVelocity - Z.targetVelocity) / safeDt, 4)
            bbPushHistory(Z.velocityHistory,     rawVelocity, 8)
            Z.targetVelocity   = rawVelocity
            Z.smoothedVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity:Lerp(rawVelocity, ZCFG.VELOCITY_SMOOTHING)
        end
        Z.lastTargetPos = targetPos

        -- Estado aéreo
        local airborne = targetHumanoid.FloorMaterial == Enum.Material.Air
        Z.airborneTime = airborne and (Z.airborneTime + safeDt) or 0
        if airborne and Z.airborneTime >= ZCFG.MIN_AIRBORNE_TIME then
            bbPushHistory(Z.aerialVelocityHistory, Z.targetVelocity, 6)
        elseif not airborne then
            Z.aerialVelocityHistory = {}
        end

        local predictionVelocity = Z.smoothedVelocity
        if airborne and #Z.aerialVelocityHistory > 0 then
            local aerial = bbAverageVector(Z.aerialVelocityHistory)
            predictionVelocity = Vector3.new(aerial.X, Z.targetVelocity.Y, aerial.Z) * ZCFG.AIR_CONTROL_FACTOR
        end

        -- Detecta cambios bruscos de dirección
        local quickTurn = false
        local horizontalVelocity = Vector3.new(Z.targetVelocity.X, 0, Z.targetVelocity.Z)
        if horizontalVelocity.Magnitude > 5 then
            local direction = horizontalVelocity.Unit
            if Z.previousDirection and Z.previousDirection:Dot(direction) < 0.5 then
                quickTurn = tick() - Z.lastDirectionChangeTime < ZCFG.DIRECTION_CHANGE_DETECTION_TIME
                Z.lastDirectionChangeTime = tick()
            end
            Z.previousDirection = direction
        end

        local serverDelay = Z.currentPing + ZCFG.SERVER_TICKRATE
        if quickTurn then serverDelay *= ZCFG.QUICK_DIRECTION_CHANGE_MULTIPLIER end

        local predicted    = targetPos + predictionVelocity * serverDelay
        local acceleration = bbAverageVector(Z.accelerationHistory)
        predicted += acceleration * ZCFG.ACCELERATION_PREDICTION_WEIGHT * (serverDelay * serverDelay * 0.5)

        local predictionTime = ZCFG.PREDICTION_TIME * 1.1
        if airborne then
            predicted += predictionVelocity * predictionTime
            predicted += Vector3.new(0, -0.5 * ZCFG.GRAVITY * predictionTime * predictionTime, 0)
        else
            predicted += predictionVelocity * predictionTime
        end

        local flatPrediction = Vector3.new(predictionVelocity.X, 0, predictionVelocity.Z)
        if flatPrediction.Magnitude > 1 then
            predicted += flatPrediction.Unit * ZCFG.PREDICT_AHEAD
        end

        local toTarget = predicted - root.Position
        bbRotateRoot(root, toTarget)

        local hitDist = tonumber(ZCFG.ACTIVATE_DISTANCE) or 13
        if (targetPos - root.Position).Magnitude <= (hitDist + 2)
        and tick() - (Z.lastActivationTime or 0) >= 0.12 then
            if bat then
                pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
                local remote = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
                if remote then pcall(function() remote:FireServer() end) end
            else
                bat = BB.FindBat()
                if bat then
                    pcall(function() humanoid:EquipTool(bat) end)
                    pcall(function() bat:Activate() end)
                end
            end
            Z.lastActivationTime = tick()
        end

        if toTarget.Magnitude > ZCFG.MIN_FOLLOW_DISTANCE then
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = toTarget.Unit * ZCFG.FOLLOW_SPEED
        else
            root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.new(0, root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y * 0.5, 0)
        end
    end)
end

function BB.Toggle()
    if BB.Enabled then BB.Stop() else BB.Start() end
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- Compat wrappers (API externa usada por UI / config / visuales)
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local function _aceSafeSave()
    if type(saveAllSettings) == "function" then pcall(saveAllSettings) end
end

local function _aceCurrentSpeedMode()
    if laggerCarryToggled then return "Lagger Carry" end
    if laggerToggled      then return "Lagger" end
    if speedMode          then return "Carry"  end
    return "Normal"
end

_G.AceGetAntiBypassAimbotSpeed = function()
    local mode = _aceCurrentSpeedMode()
    if mode == "Lagger" or mode == "Lagger Carry" then
        return tonumber(_G.AceAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed) or 40
    end
    return tonumber(_G.AceAntiBypassAimbotSpeed) or 56
end

_G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual = function()
    if _G.AceAimbotSetVisual then
        _G.AceAimbotSetVisual(_G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn == true)
    end
end

_G.AceStartAntiBypassAimbot = function()
    if _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked and _G.AmbitiousSafeModeIsLocked() then return false end
    pcall(function() if _G.AceStopNormalAimbot then _G.AceStopNormalAimbot() end end)
    pcall(stopAimbotAdapt)
    selectedAimbotMode = "Bat Bypass"
    _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn = true
    autoBatEnabled = true
    if CarrySystem then CarrySystem._batAimbotToggled = true end
    -- Restart clean
    if BB and BB.Stop then pcall(BB.Stop) end
    task.defer(function()
        local tries = 0
        while tries < 30 do
            if not _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn then return end
            local char = LP.Character
            local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
            if hum and root and BB and BB.Start then
                pcall(BB.Start)
                if BB.Connection or BB.Enabled then break end
            end
            tries = tries + 1
            task.wait(0.1)
        end
    end)
    if _G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual then pcall(_G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual) end
    pcall(function() if _aceSafeSave then _aceSafeSave() end end)
    return true
end

_G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot = function(keepVisual)
    if BB and BB.Stop then pcall(BB.Stop) end
    _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn = false
    if keepVisual ~= false and _G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual then
        _G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual()
    end
    _aceSafeSave()
end

_G.AceToggleSelectedAimbot = function()
    -- The selector chooses the mode; the mobile button/keybind controls ON/OFF.
    -- Switching to Bat Bypass already starts it automatically.
    if selectedAimbotMode == "Bat Bypass" then
        if _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn then
            _G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot()
        else
            _G.AceStartAntiBypassAimbot()
        end
    else
        -- Normal mode uses the existing normal aimbot toggle path.
        if _G.AceNormalAimbotOn then
            if _G.AceStopNormalAimbot then _G.AceStopNormalAimbot() end
            _G.AceNormalAimbotOn = false
        else
            if _G.AceStartNormalAimbot then _G.AceStartNormalAimbot() end
            _G.AceNormalAimbotOn = true
        end
    end
    if _G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual then _G.AceRefreshAimbotVisual() end
    _aceSafeSave()
end

_G.AceAntiBypassStart = _G.AceStartAntiBypassAimbot
_G.AceAntiBypassStop  = _G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot

function enableBatV2()
    selectedAimbotMode = "Off"
    if _G.AdaptBatCore then
        pcall(function() if autoBatEnabled and disableAutoBat then disableAutoBat() end end)
        _G.AdaptBatCore.bypassSpeed = (type(BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED)=="number" and BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 56
        _G.AdaptBatCore.swingDelay = 0.007
        _G.AdaptBatCore.StartBypass()
        autoBatV2Enabled = true
        _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn = true
        if autoBatV2SetVisual then autoBatV2SetVisual(true) end
        return
    end
    local ok = _G.AceStartAntiBypassAimbot()
    if ok ~= false then
        autoBatV2Enabled = true
        if autoBatV2SetVisual then autoBatV2SetVisual(true) end
        if batV2FloatingButton then
            local btnFrame = batV2FloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame")
            if btnFrame then paintFloatingBtn(btnFrame, true) end
        end
    end
end

function disableBatV2()
    if _G.AdaptBatCore then pcall(function() _G.AdaptBatCore.StopBypass() end) end
    pcall(function() if _G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot then _G.AceStopAntiBypassAimbot() end end)
    autoBatV2Enabled = false
    if autoBatV2SetVisual then autoBatV2SetVisual(false) end
    if batV2FloatingButton then
        local btnFrame = batV2FloatingButton:FindFirstChild("Frame")
        if btnFrame then paintFloatingBtn(btnFrame, false) end
    end
end

function toggleBatV2()
    if autoBatV2Enabled then disableBatV2() else enableBatV2() end
end

_G.AceAntiBypassSaveToConfig = function(t)
    t = t or {}
    t.selectedAimbotMode              = selectedAimbotMode
    t.ANTI_BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED        = _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotSpeed
    t.ANTI_BYPASS_LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = _G.AceAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed
    t.antiBypassAimbotEnabled         = _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn == true
    return t
end

_G.AceAntiBypassLoadFromConfig = function(data)
    if type(data) ~= "table" then return end
    selectedAimbotMode = data.selectedAimbotMode or selectedAimbotMode
    -- Config migration: keep only Normal / Bat Bypass.
    if selectedAimbotMode == "Anti Bypass" or selectedAimbotMode == "__REMOVED_BAT_BYPASS__" or selectedAimbotMode == "Off" then
        selectedAimbotMode = "Normal"
    elseif selectedAimbotMode ~= "Bat Bypass" then
        selectedAimbotMode = "Normal"
    end
    _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotSpeed =
        tonumber(data.ANTI_BYPASS_AIMBOT_SPEED) or _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotSpeed or 56

    if data.ANTI_BYPASS_LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED == nil
       or tonumber(data.ANTI_BYPASS_LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) == 58 then
        _G.AceAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed = 40
    else
        _G.AceAntiBypassLaggerAimbotSpeed =
            tonumber(data.ANTI_BYPASS_LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED) or 40
    end

    _G.AceAntiBypassAimbotOn = data.antiBypassAimbotEnabled == true
end

-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
-- TP BAT — swapped in from tpbat.txt
-- Spider.vs compatibility wrapper
-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local RunService = game:GetService("RunService")
local LP = Players.LocalPlayer

local mode = "Off"

-- Shared TP Bat controls
local TPBAT_DIST = 3
local TPBAT_SPAM_JUMP = false

local function tpBatGetOffset(targetHRP)
    if not targetHRP then return nil end
    -- TP Bat body-lock: center directly on the target's HumanoidRootPart.
    -- This intentionally ignores Dist so all V1/V2/V3 stay inside the target.
    return targetHRP.CFrame
end

local function tpBatSpamJump(humanoid)
    if not TPBAT_SPAM_JUMP or not humanoid then return end
    pcall(function()
        humanoid.Jump = true
        humanoid:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
    end)
end

_G.fa4e7xxTPBatSetDist = function(value)
    local n = tonumber(value)
    if not n then return end
    TPBAT_DIST = math.clamp(n, 0, 3000)
end

_G.fa4e7xxTPBatSetSpamJump = function(on)
    TPBAT_SPAM_JUMP = (on == true)
end

local V1_CONFIG = {
    Range = 250,
    TPOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3.2, 0),
    LoopDelay = 0.02,
    SmartRotation = true,
    ResetVelocity = true,
    PredictMovement = true,
    PredictionFactor = 0.15,
    HealthCheck = true,
    TeamCheck = false,
    BehindTarget = false,
    BehindDistance = 4,
    AntiFling = true,
    SafeDistance = 3,
    SideOffset = 0,
    AutoBatEnabled = true,
    AutoBatDelay = 0.08,
    SpinSpeed = 32,
    OrbitRadius = 0.45,
    OrbitHeight = 1.0
}

local V1 = {
    Enabled = false,
    isRunning = false,
    tpLoopThread = nil,
    hittingCooldown = false,
    humanoid = nil,
    hrp = nil,
    oldWalkSpeed = nil,
    oldJumpPower = nil,
    oldAutoRotate = nil,
    oldCameraType = nil,
    oldCameraSubject = nil,
    cameraConn = nil,
    spin = 0
}

local function v1GetMyHRP()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function v1GetMyHumanoid()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function v1GetBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end

    local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
    if tool then return tool end

    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        tool = bp:FindFirstChild("Bat")
        if tool then
            tool.Parent = char
            return tool
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function v1TryHitBat()
    if V1.hittingCooldown then return end
    V1.hittingCooldown = true

    pcall(function()
        local bat = v1GetBat()
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then
                ev:FireServer()
            end
        end
    end)

    task.delay(V1_CONFIG.AutoBatDelay, function()
        V1.hittingCooldown = false
    end)
end

local function v1IsValidEnemy(player)
    if player == LP then return false end
    if not player.Character then return false end

    local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return false end
    if V1_CONFIG.HealthCheck and humanoid.Health <= 0 then return false end

    if V1_CONFIG.TeamCheck then
        if player.Team == LP.Team and player.Team ~= nil then
            return false
        end
    end

    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return false end

    return true, hrp, humanoid
end

local function v1PredictPosition(hrp)
    if not V1_CONFIG.PredictMovement then
        return hrp.Position
    end

    return hrp.Position + (hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity * V1_CONFIG.PredictionFactor)
end

local function v1FindNearestEnemy()
    local myHRP = v1GetMyHRP()
    if not myHRP then return nil end

    local nearestHRP = nil
    local shortestDist = V1_CONFIG.Range

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local valid, hrp = v1IsValidEnemy(player)

        if valid then
            local dist = (hrp.Position - myHRP.Position).Magnitude

            if dist < shortestDist then
                shortestDist = dist
                nearestHRP = hrp
            end
        end
    end

    return nearestHRP
end

local function v1RestoreMovement()
    if V1.humanoid and V1.humanoid.Parent then
        pcall(function()
            if V1.oldWalkSpeed ~= nil then
                V1.humanoid.WalkSpeed = V1.oldWalkSpeed
            end

            if V1.oldJumpPower ~= nil then
                V1.humanoid.JumpPower = V1.oldJumpPower
            end

            if V1.oldAutoRotate ~= nil then
                V1.humanoid.AutoRotate = V1.oldAutoRotate
            end

            V1.humanoid.PlatformStand = false
            V1.humanoid.Sit = false
        end)
    end

    V1.oldWalkSpeed = nil
    V1.oldJumpPower = nil
    V1.oldAutoRotate = nil
end

local function v1TeleportTo(targetHRP)
    local myHRP = v1GetMyHRP()
    local myHumanoid = v1GetMyHumanoid()

    if not myHRP or not myHumanoid or not targetHRP then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        myHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
        myHumanoid.JumpPower = TPBAT_SPAM_JUMP and 50 or 0
        myHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
        myHumanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)
        tpBatSpamJump(myHumanoid)
        myHumanoid.PlatformStand = false
        myHumanoid.Sit = false

        -- Exact body lock: center directly on the target HumanoidRootPart.
        myHRP.CFrame = tpBatGetOffset(targetHRP)

        myHRP.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        myHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        myHRP.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        myHRP.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

local function v1UpdateCamera()
    local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
    local character = LP.Character
    local humanoid = character and character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not camera or not humanoid then return end

    pcall(function()
        camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
        camera.CameraSubject = humanoid
    end)
end

local function v1Start()
    if V1.isRunning then return end

    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return end

    local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    local hrp = char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")

    if not humanoid or not hrp then return end

    V1.humanoid = humanoid
    V1.hrp = hrp
    V1.oldWalkSpeed = humanoid.WalkSpeed
    V1.oldJumpPower = humanoid.JumpPower
    V1.oldAutoRotate = humanoid.AutoRotate
    V1.oldCameraType = workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraType
    V1.oldCameraSubject = workspace.CurrentCamera.CameraSubject
    V1.spin = 0

    V1.cameraConn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
        if not V1.Enabled then return end

        local target = v1FindNearestEnemy()
        if target then
            v1UpdateCamera()
        end
    end)
    V1.Enabled = true
    V1.isRunning = true

    pcall(function()
        humanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
        humanoid.JumpPower = 0
        humanoid.AutoRotate = true
        humanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)
        hrp.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        hrp.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)

    V1.tpLoopThread = task.spawn(function()
        while V1.Enabled do
            local target = v1FindNearestEnemy()

            if target then
                v1TeleportTo(target)

                if V1_CONFIG.AutoBatEnabled then
                    v1TryHitBat()
                end
            else
                local currentHRP = v1GetMyHRP()
                local currentHumanoid = v1GetMyHumanoid()

                if currentHRP and currentHumanoid then
                    pcall(function()
                        currentHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
                        currentHumanoid.JumpPower = 0
                        currentHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
                        currentHumanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)
                        currentHRP.Velocity = Vector3.zero
                        currentHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        currentHRP.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                    end)
                end
            end

            task.wait(V1_CONFIG.LoopDelay)
        end
    end)
end

local function v1Stop()
    if not V1.isRunning then return end

    V1.Enabled = false
    V1.isRunning = false

    if V1.tpLoopThread then
        pcall(function()
            task.cancel(V1.tpLoopThread)
        end)
        V1.tpLoopThread = nil
    end

    if V1.cameraConn then
        V1.cameraConn:Disconnect()
        V1.cameraConn = nil
    end

    pcall(function()
        local camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
        if camera then
            if V1.oldCameraType then
                camera.CameraType = V1.oldCameraType
            else
                camera.CameraType = Enum.CameraType.Custom
            end

            if V1.oldCameraSubject then
                camera.CameraSubject = V1.oldCameraSubject
            end
        end
    end)

    v1RestoreMovement()

    V1.humanoid = nil
    V1.hrp = nil
    V1.hittingCooldown = false
    V1.spin = 0
end

local function v1Toggle()
    if V1.Enabled then
        v1Stop()
    else
        v1Start()
    end
end

_G.fa4e7xxTPBatV1Toggle = v1Toggle
_G.fa4e7xxTPBatV1Start = v1Start
_G.fa4e7xxTPBatV1Stop = v1Stop

local V2 = {
    enabled = false,
    cooldown = false,
    loop = nil
}

local V2_CONFIG = {
    Range = 250,
    TPOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0),
    LoopDelay = 0.08,
    SmartRotation = false,
    ResetVelocity = true,
    PredictMovement = false,
    PredictionFactor = 0,
    HealthCheck = true,
    TeamCheck = false,
    BehindTarget = false,
    BehindDistance = 0,
    AntiFling = false,
    SafeDistance = 0,
    SideOffset = 0,
    AutoBatEnabled = true,
    AutoBatDelay = 0.08
}

local function v2GetHRP()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
end

local function v2GetHumanoid()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end
    return char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
end

local function v2GetBat()
    local char = LP.Character
    if not char then return nil end

    local tool = char:FindFirstChild("Bat")
    if tool then return tool end

    local bp = LP:FindFirstChild("Backpack")
    if bp then
        tool = bp:FindFirstChild("Bat")
        if tool then
            tool.Parent = char
            return tool
        end
    end

    return nil
end

local function v2TryHitBat()
    if V2.cooldown then return end
    V2.cooldown = true

    pcall(function()
        local bat = v2GetBat()
        if bat then
            bat:Activate()
            local ev = bat:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("RemoteEvent")
            if ev then
                ev:FireServer()
            end
        end
    end)

    task.delay(V2_CONFIG.AutoBatDelay, function()
        V2.cooldown = false
    end)
end

local function v2ValidEnemy(player)
    if player == LP then return false end
    if not player.Character then return false end

    local humanoid = player.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
    if not humanoid then return false end

    if V2_CONFIG.HealthCheck and humanoid.Health <= 0 then
        return false
    end

    if V2_CONFIG.TeamCheck and player.Team == LP.Team and player.Team ~= nil then
        return false
    end

    local hrp = player.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not hrp then return false end

    return true, hrp, humanoid
end

local function v2PredictPosition(hrp)
    if not V2_CONFIG.PredictMovement then
        return hrp.Position
    end

    return hrp.Position + hrp.AssemblyLinearVelocity * V2_CONFIG.PredictionFactor
end

local function v2FindNearestEnemy()
    local myHRP = v2GetHRP()
    if not myHRP then return nil end

    local nearestHRP
    local shortestDist = V2_CONFIG.Range

    for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        local valid, hrp = v2ValidEnemy(player)

        if valid then
            local dist = (hrp.Position - myHRP.Position).Magnitude

            if dist < shortestDist then
                shortestDist = dist
                nearestHRP = hrp
            end
        end
    end

    return nearestHRP
end

local function v2TeleportTo(targetHRP)
    local myHRP = v2GetHRP()
    local myHumanoid = v2GetHumanoid()

    if not myHRP or not targetHRP then
        return
    end

    pcall(function()
        if myHumanoid then
            myHumanoid.WalkSpeed = 0
            myHumanoid.JumpPower = TPBAT_SPAM_JUMP and 50 or 0
            myHumanoid.AutoRotate = false
            myHumanoid:Move(Vector3.zero)
            tpBatSpamJump(myHumanoid)
            myHumanoid.PlatformStand = false
            myHumanoid.Sit = false
        end

        -- Exact body lock: center directly on the target HumanoidRootPart.
        myHRP.CFrame = tpBatGetOffset(targetHRP)

        myHRP.Velocity = Vector3.zero
        myHRP.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
        myHRP.RotVelocity = Vector3.zero
        myHRP.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
    end)
end

local function v2Stop()
    V2.enabled = false
    V2.cooldown = false

    if V2.loop then
        pcall(function()
            task.cancel(V2.loop)
        end)
        V2.loop = nil
    end
end

local function v2Start()
    v2Stop()
    V2.enabled = true

    V2.loop = task.spawn(function()
        while V2.enabled and mode == "V2" do
            local target = v2FindNearestEnemy()

            if target then
                v2TeleportTo(target)

                if V2_CONFIG.AutoBatEnabled then
                    v2TryHitBat()
                end
            end

            task.wait(V2_CONFIG.LoopDelay)
        end
    end)
end
